) we can estimate two derivatives of
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
by one derivative of
\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}
at the cost of the loss of one
\lambda
power. This would however prove well-posedness of (
2.58
)-(
2.59
) only on a time scale of order
\lambda
, which is far from satisfactory if one is interested in the limit
\lambda\to 0
. Therefore, one absolutely needs to use the structure of the term
\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
on the RHS of (
2.59
). This is done by decomposing it as follows

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}-\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\mu\nu}\right)\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}.

(4.6)

We explain how the two terms in (
4.6
) can be estimated:

•

The first term in (
4.6
) can be estimated by commuting (
2.58
) and using the estimate (proved in Appendix B of
[
Tou23a
]
)

\displaystyle\left\|[L_{\mathbf{A}},\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}]f\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(C_{0})\left(\left\|\partial L_{\mathbf{A}}f\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}f\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\partial f\right\|_{L^{2}}\right),

where it is crucial that the only second order derivatives appearing on the RHS include a
L_{\mathbf{A}}
or are directly a wave operator. This implies that
\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
can be estimated roughly by
\partial\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}
and
\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\Pi_{\leq}(\mathfrak{h})
. The former is itself controlled by
\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
thanks to (
2.59
) and the latter requires to commute
\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}
and
\Pi_{\leq}
. In Appendix C of
[
Tou23a
]
we prove the following estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\left[u,\Pi_{\leq}\right]\nabla v\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\lambda\left\|u\right\|_{H^{\frac{7}{2}}}\right)\left\|v\right\|_{L^{2}}.

This estimates shows in particular how one gain a derivative at top order.

•

For the second term in (
4.6
), instead of relying on the structure of the derivatives we benefit from the fact that
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}-\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\mu\nu}=O\left(\lambda\right)
. This extra factor
\lambda
makes now possible the use of the Bernstein estimate (
4.5
).

Integrating this procedure in a bootstrap argument with (
4.2
)-(
4.4
) as bootstrap assumptions allows us to prove well-posedness for the system (
2.58
)-(
2.59
) and obtain a unique solution
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}
satisfying (
4.2
)-(
4.4
) on the desired time scale. This concludes our outline of the proof of Proposition
4.1
, and we again refer to
[
Tou23a
]
for all the technical details.

5
Propagation of polarization and gauge conditions

In this section, we conclude the proof of Theorem
1.3
by showing that the metric
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
defined by (
2.1
) where
\left(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}},F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}},F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}},\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}},\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}\right)
are constructed in Section
4
and where
F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
,
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T}
and
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T}
are respectively defined in (
2.68
), (
2.69
) and Remark
2.5
, is a solution of the Einstein vacuum equations (
1.1
). As first stated in Section
2.4
, this will be done by showing that the algebraic conditions (
2.60
)-(
2.67
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
((
2.65
) already holds thanks to the definition of
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T}
, see Remark
2.5
), relying obviously on the fact that they all hold on
\Sigma_{0}
thanks to Proposition
3.2
. These conditions are separated in two groups:

•

First, the conditions on
F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
, i.e (
2.60
)-(
2.62
), (
2.66
), which are all propagated by directly using the transport equations that
F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
satisfy on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, i.e (
2.55
) and (
2.58
). This is done in Section
5.1
.

•

Second, the conditions on
F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
, i.e (
2.63
)-(
2.64
), together with the generalised wave gauge condition (
2.67
), which are propagated using the contracted Bianchi identities. This is done in Section
5.2
.

Remark 5.1
.

As explained in Remark
2.2
, the conditions (
2.63
)-(
2.64
) are not gauge conditions since they don’t depend on coordinates. However we treat them as gauge conditions and propagate them with the contracted Bianchi identities.

5.1
Propagation via the system

The proof of the following lemma is identical to the corresponding ones in
[
Tou23a
]
, we only sketch its proof and refer the reader to Section 8.1 in
[
Tou23a
]
for more details.

Lemma 5.1
.

The polarization conditions (
2.60
)-(
2.62
) and the backreaction condition (
2.66
) hold on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Proof.

By contracting the transport equation (
2.55
) with various vector fields we obtain the transport system

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle=0,

(5.1)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{e^{(\mathbf{k})}_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{g}_{0}\left(\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}},e^{(\mathbf{k})}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\mathbf{i},\mathbf{j}=1,2}\delta_{\mathbf{i}\mathbf{j}}\mathbf{g}_{0}\left(\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}e^{(\mathbf{k})}_{\mathbf{A}},e^{(\mathbf{i})}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{e^{(\mathbf{j})}_{\mathbf{A}}},

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-2\sum_{\mathbf{i},\mathbf{j}=1,2}\delta_{\mathbf{i}\mathbf{j}}\mathbf{g}_{0}\left(\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}},e^{(\mathbf{i})}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{e^{(\mathbf{j})}_{\mathbf{A}}},

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\mathbf{A}}\left((F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\mathbf{i},\mathbf{j}=1,2}\delta_{\mathbf{i}\mathbf{j}}\mathbf{g}_{0}\left(\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}},e^{(\mathbf{i})}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{e^{(\mathbf{j})}_{\mathbf{A}}}.

Since
\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
and
(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}\underline{L}_{\mathbf{A}}|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
, the system (
5.1
) implies that (
2.60
)-(
2.61
) hold on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Similarly, using now (
2.60
)-(
2.61
) we deduce from (
2.55
) the following transport equation

\displaystyle\left(-L_{\mathbf{A}}+\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle=0.

Using now the background transport equation satisfied by
F_{\mathbf{A}}
(recall (
1.11
)), we obtain

\displaystyle\left(-L_{\mathbf{A}}+\Box_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\left(\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}^{2}-8F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=0.

(5.2)

Since we have

\displaystyle\left(\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}^{2}-8F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\right)_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0,

the equation (
5.2
) implies that (
2.66
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Finally, using again (
2.60
) we deduce from (
2.58
) that
\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]
satisfies the same system as
\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]
, i.e (
5.1
) with
F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
replaced by
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
. Since
\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
, we again obtain that (
2.62
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, which concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

5.2
Propagation via the contracted Bianchi identities

We now propagate from
\Sigma_{0}
the conditions (
2.63
)-(
2.64
) and (
2.67
). This is done by deriving extra equations for the quantities
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\Upsilon^{\rho}
from the contracted Bianchi identities, which state that the Einstein tensor of any Lorentzian metric is divergence free. We first compute the Einstein tensor of
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
.

Lemma 5.2
.

The Einstein tensor of
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
admits the decomposition

\displaystyle G_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=G(V)_{\alpha\beta}+G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}.

The term
G(V)_{\alpha\beta}
is given by

\displaystyle G(V)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=G^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda G^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}+O\left(\lambda^{2}\right),

(5.3)

where

\displaystyle G^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V_{\mathbf{A}}^{(2,1)})_{\beta)}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(5.4)

\displaystyle\quad-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V_{\mathbf{A}}^{(2,2)})_{\beta)}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right),

\displaystyle G^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}-\mathrm{div}_{g_{0}}V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(5.5)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}-\mathrm{div}_{g_{0}}V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{A},k=1,2,3\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{ku_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)}\right\}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}.

The term
G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}
is given by

\displaystyle G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\bigg((\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho}-(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\rho}\Upsilon^{\rho}+\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}\bigg).

(5.6)

Proof.

Thanks to Lemma
5.1
, the assumptions (
2.6
) are satisfied and we can use the results of Proposition
2.1
. Thanks to Lemma
5.1
again, the non-oscillating term in (
2.29
) vanish and thanks to (
2.31
), (
2.68
) and Lemma
2.4
we have
(R^{(0)}_{\mathrm{mixed}})_{\alpha\beta}=0
. Therefore we have
R^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}=(R^{(0)}_{\mathrm{null}})_{\alpha\beta}
. Moreover, the transport equation (
2.55
), the polarization condition (
2.62
) (which holds thanks to Lemma
5.1
) and the definitions (
2.43
)-(
2.44
) imply that

\displaystyle R^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}.

(5.7)

We now compute
R^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}
. The definition (
2.69
) of
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T}
precisely ensures that
(R^{(1)}_{\mathrm{mixed}})_{\alpha\beta}=0
so that

\displaystyle R^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}=(R^{(1)}_{\mathrm{null}})_{\alpha\beta}+\frac{1}{2}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}(\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho})^{(-1)}.

(5.8)

Moreover, the transport equations (
2.56
)-(
2.58
) and the conditions (
2.62
) and (
2.65
) imply

\displaystyle(R^{(1)}_{\mathrm{null}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(-\Pi_{\geq}\left((\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\bigg(2\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)-\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg)(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta},

where we used (
2.49
). Finally, thanks to the wave equation (
2.59
) we have from (
2.41
):

\displaystyle R^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\lambda}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Pi_{\geq}\left((\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho}\right)^{(\geq 0)}.

(5.10)

Combining (
5.7
)-(
5.10
) we obtain the decomposition

\displaystyle R_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=R(V)_{\alpha\beta}+R(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}

with

\displaystyle R(V)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lambda}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}-\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{D}_{(\alpha}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}

(5.11)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\lambda}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\bigg(2\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)-\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg)(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta},

\displaystyle R(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\left(\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho}\right).

(5.12)

We define

\displaystyle G(V)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=R(V)_{\alpha\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}R(V)_{\mu\nu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta},

\displaystyle G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=R(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}R(\Upsilon)_{\mu\nu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta},

so that the Einstein tensor of
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
satisfies
G_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})=G(V)_{\alpha\beta}+G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}
. Moreover,
G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha\beta}
is indeed given by (
5.6
). It remains to compute
G(V)_{\alpha\beta}
, for that we compute the trace
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}R(V)_{\mu\nu}
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}R(V)_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}+4\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathrm{div}_{g_{0}}V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-2\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathrm{div}_{g_{0}}V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(1)}(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})\right\}+O\left(\lambda^{2}\right).

Plugging this into the definition of
G(V)_{\alpha\beta}
, we obtain the decomposition (
5.3
).
∎

The contracted Bianchi identities applied to the metric
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
now read

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(\Upsilon)

\displaystyle=0.

(5.13)

To deduce extra equations from (
5.13
) we compute the divergence of
G(\Upsilon)
and
G(V)
in the two following lemmas.

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
K
be any compact set of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(\Upsilon)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}+\mathcal{R}_{1}

with

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{R}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}(K)}\lesssim\lambda.

(5.14)

Proof.

From (
5.6
) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(\Upsilon)_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\left((\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho\alpha}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\Upsilon^{\rho}+\mathcal{B}_{\alpha}\right)

where
\mathcal{B}_{\alpha}
is schematically of the form

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}

\displaystyle=\left(1+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\Upsilon\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\Upsilon\partial^{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\Upsilon\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right).

Since
\Upsilon=O\left(1\right)
and
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}=O\left(1\right)
both with oscillating coefficients, the worst terms in
\mathcal{B}
are
\partial\Upsilon
and
\partial^{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
, and note that they are not multiplied together. Using the regularity and the estimates stated in Proposition
4.1
, we conclude easily that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{B}\right\|_{L^{2}(K)}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}.

We now look at the term
\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\Upsilon^{\rho}
. From (
2.23
) we obtain the following schematic expression of
\Upsilon^{\rho}
:

\displaystyle\Upsilon

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left\{\partial\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}\right\}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(1)}\right\}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}+\text{better terms},

where for clarity we choose not to write the
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}
factors (which are lower order terms from the point of view of the
\lambda
behaviour) and where the better terms includes terms with the same amounts of derivatives but with better
\lambda
behaviour (for instance, terms of the form
\lambda\partial\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}
or
\lambda\partial\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}
). Therefore, using (
A.8
) we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\Upsilon

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left\{\partial\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}\right\}+\left\{\partial\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{F}+\partial^{2}F^{(1)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\left\{\partial L_{\mathbf{A}}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+\partial^{\leq 1}F^{(1)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}

(5.15)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}+\left\{\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}+\text{better terms},

where we used the fact that the commutators
\left[\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}},\partial\right]
and
[L_{\mathbf{A}},\partial]
are better terms. Compared to the singlephase construction of
[
Tou23a
]
, the only new term in (
5.15
) is

\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\},

which we keep as it is. The other terms can be estimated as in Lemma 8.2 of
[
Tou23a
]
(using in particular the fact that the worst term in
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}
is a linear term in
\mathfrak{F}
), we don’t write the details and obtain that there are all bounded by
\frac{1}{\lambda}
in
L^{2}(K)
. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma 5.4
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathcal{R}_{2})_{\alpha}

with

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{R}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}(K)}\lesssim\lambda.

(5.16)

Proof.

Since
G(V)
contains oscillating terms at the order
\lambda^{0}
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}\right)^{(-1)}+\left(\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}\right)^{(0)}+O\left(\lambda\right).

Recall that if
T
is a symmetric 2-tensor we have in coordinates

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}T_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\beta}\partial_{\rho}T_{\alpha\beta}-\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\beta}\Gamma(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}^{\mu}T_{\mu\beta)}.

Therefore, we have thanks to Lemma
5.2
:

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial^{\beta}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V_{\mathbf{A}}^{(2,1)})_{\beta)}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+4\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial^{\beta}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(V_{\mathbf{A}}^{(2,2)})_{\beta)}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle=0.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}G(V)_{\alpha}\right)^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathrm{div}_{g_{0}}G^{(0)}_{\alpha}\right)^{(0)}+\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\beta}\left(\partial_{\rho}G^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}\right)^{(-1)}+\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\beta}\right)^{(1)}\left(\partial_{\rho}G(V)_{\alpha\beta}\right)^{(-1)}-\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\beta}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})_{\rho(\alpha}^{\mu}G^{(0)}_{\mu\beta)}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=I+II+III+IV,

where

\displaystyle(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})_{\rho\alpha}^{\mu}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{B}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{(\rho}u_{\mathbf{B}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha)}^{\mu}-\partial^{\mu}u_{\mathbf{B}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\rho\alpha}\right).

For
I
, (
5.4
) implies

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big((L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}\mathbf{D}_{\rho}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}-\partial_{\alpha}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big((L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}\mathbf{D}_{\rho}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}-\partial_{\alpha}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}\Big).

For
II
, (
5.5
) implies

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-L_{\mathbf{A}}^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{\alpha}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta}-\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}-\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-L_{\mathbf{A}}^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{\alpha}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta}-\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}-\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}

For
III
and
IV
, (
5.4
) simply implies

\displaystyle III+IV

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}.

Putting everything together, we obtain the lemma, using in particular the fact that all the
\lambda
terms in
G(V)
are oscillating with amplitudes depending only on
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
F^{(1)}
which can be bounded independently of
\lambda
, thus producing the remainder term
\mathcal{R}_{2}
.
∎

Lemma 5.5
.

The polarization conditions (
2.63
)-(
2.64
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Proof.

Thanks to the contracted Bianchi identities (
5.13
) and Lemmas
5.3
and
5.4
we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}_{\alpha}+\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2}.

We multiply this equality by
\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
and obtain

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sin^{2}\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{B}}+2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{B}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha]}\right)

(5.17)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{\mathbf{B}\},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}V^{(2,1)}\right\}_{\alpha}+\sum_{\mathbf{A},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}\pm 2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}V^{(2,2)}\right\}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(V^{(2,1)}+V^{(2,2)})F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2}\right).

We will use the following claim: for all
T:\mathbb{R}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}
smooth,
2\pi
-periodic with
\int_{0}^{2\pi}T=0
and for all
z\in\mathcal{Z}
the sequence of functions
\left(T\left(\frac{z}{\lambda}\right)\right)_{\lambda\in(0,1]}
converges weakly to 0 in
L^{2}(K)
when
\lambda\to 0
and where
K
is any compact subset of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. This follows from (
1.19
) and was already mentioned in Section
1.5
. Here, we use this claim on each
\Sigma_{t}
for
t\in[0,1]
and
K=\Sigma_{t}\cap J^{+}_{0}(B_{R})
, i.e the support of
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
. Thus, we have the following weak limits in
L^{2}(K)
when
\lambda\to 0
:

\displaystyle\sin^{2}\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\rightharpoonup\frac{1}{2},

\displaystyle\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\rightharpoonup 0,

\displaystyle\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}\pm 2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\rightharpoonup 0,

\displaystyle T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\rightharpoonup 0,

where we used that
T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
with
v\in\mathcal{I}
and
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}
is a linear combination of functions of the form
S\left(\frac{z}{\lambda}\right)
for
z\in\mathcal{Z}
and
S
periodic with zero mean. Since
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
is uniformly bounded in
H^{1}_{loc}
with respect to
\lambda
(see (
4.2
)), we still get the weak convergence

\displaystyle T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}\rightharpoonup 0,

where we again used the fact that the worst term in
\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}
is a linear term in
\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}
. Using now (
5.14
) and (
5.16
), we can take the weak limit in
L^{2}(K)
when
\lambda\to 0
of the equality (
5.17
) and get for all
\mathbf{B}\in\mathcal{A}

\displaystyle 2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{B}}}(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\alpha}(L_{\mathbf{B}})_{\beta]}-(V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{B}}.

Since
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}\;\;|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
this implies
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}}=0
everywhere, i.e that (
2.63
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. We come back to the contracted Bianchi identities (plugging in particular
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{C}}=0
):

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{V^{(2,2)}F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}_{\alpha}+\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2}.

We multiply this equality by
\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=2\cos^{2}\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{B}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{B}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{\mathbf{B}\},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{2(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})}{\lambda}\right)\Big(2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{A}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha}+(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\beta}(L_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha]}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{V^{(2,2)}F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2}\right).

Using the claim as above, taking the weak limit in
L^{2}(K)
when
\lambda\to 0
of this expression implies

\displaystyle 2\mathbf{D}_{L_{\mathbf{C}}}(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{C}})_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{C}})^{\beta}\mathbf{D}_{[\alpha}(L_{\mathbf{C}})_{\beta]}-(V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{C}})_{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}L_{\mathbf{C}}.

Since
V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{C}\;\;|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
this implies
V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{C}}=0
everywhere. This concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 5.6
.

The generalised wave gauge condition (
2.67
) holds on
[0,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Proof.

Thanks to the previous proposition we have
V^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}=V^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}=0
so that (
5.11
) implies that
R(V)=0
and that the Einstein tensor of
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
is given by

\displaystyle G_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\bigg((\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho}-(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\rho}\Upsilon^{\rho}+\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\Upsilon^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}\bigg).

(5.18)

From there, it is standard to deduce from the contracted Bianchi identities that
\Upsilon^{\rho}
satisfies a linear system of the form

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\Upsilon^{\rho}

\displaystyle=A^{\rho\mu}_{\sigma}\partial_{\mu}\Upsilon^{\sigma}+B^{\rho}_{\sigma}\Upsilon^{\sigma},

(5.19)

with
A^{\rho\mu}_{\sigma}
and
B^{\rho}_{\sigma}
regular coefficients. From Proposition
3.2
recall that
\Upsilon^{\rho}_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
. Moreover again from Proposition
3.2
we know that the constraint equations are solved by the induced metric and the second fundamental form so that
G(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
and
G(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}i|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
, where
\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}
is the future-directed unit normal for
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
to
\Sigma_{0}
. From (
5.18
) we have

\displaystyle G(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}i|_{\Sigma_{0}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho i}\left(\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}-\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}^{\rho}\partial_{t}\Upsilon^{0}\right),

\displaystyle G(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}|_{\Sigma_{0}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\left(2(\mathbf{T}_{\lambda})_{\rho}\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}+\partial_{t}\Upsilon^{0}\right),

where we used
\Upsilon^{\rho}_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
. The constraint equations being solved thus implies

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}^{\rho}\partial_{t}\Upsilon^{0},

(5.20)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Upsilon^{0}

\displaystyle=-2(\mathbf{T}_{\lambda})_{\rho}\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}.

(5.21)

Thanks to (
5.20
) and
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}(\mathbf{T}_{\lambda},\mathbf{T}_{\lambda})=-1
, (
5.21
) implies
\partial_{t}\Upsilon^{0}=0
so that (
5.20
) becomes in turn
\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}=0
. Therefore,
\Upsilon^{\rho}
satisfies the linear wave system (
5.19
) and
\Upsilon^{\rho}_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=\mathbf{T}_{\lambda}\Upsilon^{\rho}_{|_{\Sigma_{0}}}=0
, which concludes the proof of the proposition.
∎

This concludes the proof of Theorem
1.3
, after the identifications

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}_{1}\cup\mathcal{N}_{2}\cup\mathcal{I}_{2}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)F^{(2,w,T)}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)+\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T}+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T}.

The estimates (
1.31
)-(
1.33
) can then be derived from (
4.1
)-(
4.2
) and (
4.4
).

Appendix A
Proofs of Section
2

In the following sections we will use the following trigonometric identities:

\displaystyle\cos(a)\cos(b)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\cos(a\pm b),

(A.1)

\displaystyle\sin(a)\sin(b)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\mp\cos(a\pm b),

(A.2)

\displaystyle\sin(a)\cos(b)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\pm}\sin(a\pm b).

(A.3)

We will also use the following identities on double sums:

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}+F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}}\right),

(A.4)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\pm F^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}}\right),

(A.5)

which simply follow from

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B}}T_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B}}(T_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}+T_{\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}}).

Moreover, from (
2.1
) we can obtain the inverse of
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\mu\nu}+\lambda(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(2)}+\lambda^{3}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(\geq 3)},

where

\displaystyle(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(1)}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu},

(A.6)

\displaystyle(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(2)}

\displaystyle=-\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu}\right)+\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu}\right)

(A.7)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\nu\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\mu}_{\sigma}-(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})^{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos^{2}\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\nu\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu}_{\sigma},

and where on the RHS of (
A.6
) and (
A.7
) the indexes are moved with respect to the background metric
\mathbf{g}_{0}
.

A.1
Proof of Lemma
2.1

The expansion of the quasi-linear wave operator
\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}
follows from a systematic use of the exact formula

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{g}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)f\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}T^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)g^{-1}(\mathrm{d}v,\mathrm{d}v)f+\frac{1}{\lambda}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(2g^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}v\partial_{\nu}f+(\tilde{\Box}_{g}v)f\right)+T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{g}f,

(A.8)

which holds for any Lorentzian metric
g
, real function
T
and scalar functions on the manifold
v
and
f
. From (
A.6
) and (
A.7
) we can also obtain the expansion

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{A}},\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle=-\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{B}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}

(A.9)

\displaystyle-\lambda^{2}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}-\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{B}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle-\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{B}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{B}\neq\mathbf{C},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}\pm u_{\mathbf{C}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\sigma}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{C}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}\sigma}-(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{B}\mathbf{C}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{B}}\cos^{2}\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\sigma}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}\sigma}+\lambda^{3}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu})^{(\geq 3)}\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\nu}u_{\mathbf{A}}.

Thanks to (
2.1
) we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(A.10)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta}\right).

We expand each term in this expression, using without mention (
A.8
), (
A.9
) and more generally (
A.6
) and (
A.7
). We will also use without mention the assumptions (
2.6
). For the first term, we simply have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda\left\{(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 1)}\partial^{2}\mathbf{g}_{0}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}.

(A.11)

For the second term, we first apply (
A.8
)

\displaystyle\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\lambda}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{A}},\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{A}})(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(2\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\nu}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}u_{\mathbf{A}})(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}.

Using now (
A.9
), (
A.1
), (
A.3
) and (
A.4
) we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(A.12)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-2L_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}})(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{B}}L_{\mathbf{B}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+\left\{\left(F^{(2,\pm)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}\right)F^{(1)}+\partial^{2}F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(F^{(2,1)}+\partial F^{(1)}+F^{(1)}\right)F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\Big\{\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}\right)F^{(1)}

\displaystyle+\left((F^{(1)})^{2}+F^{(1)}+\partial F^{(1)}\right)F^{(1)}\Big\}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\left\{(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 2)}\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

For the third, fourth and fifth term in (
A.10
) we simply apply (
A.8
). We obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)\right)

(A.13)

\displaystyle=\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-2L_{\mathbf{A}}+\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}\right)F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\left\{(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 1)}\partial^{\leq 1}\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}\right)+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}F^{(2,1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}},

and

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(A.14)

\displaystyle=-2\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-2L_{\mathbf{A}}+\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(2,2)}F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda^{2}\left\{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(2,2)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}},

and

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(A.15)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}),\mathrm{d}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}))(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm_{1}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(2,\pm_{1})}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}F^{(2,\pm)}+F^{(1)}F^{(2,\pm)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\lambda^{2}\left\{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(2,\pm)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

The sixth term is left as it is. For the seventh and eighth we apply (
A.8
) and obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle=\lambda^{2}\left\{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}}

(A.16)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\alpha\beta},

and

\displaystyle\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle=-\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}v,\mathrm{d}v)(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta}

(A.17)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\left\{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta},

where we used that
T^{\prime\prime}=-T
if
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}
. Collecting (
A.11
)-(
A.17
) concludes the proof of Lemma
2.1
.

A.2
Proof of Lemma
2.2

We recall (
2.5
), which gives the expression of
P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda},\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})
. It is of the form
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}
so that schematically we have

\displaystyle\left(P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda},\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}.

Thanks to (
2.1
) we have

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{\alpha}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}\right)^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\alpha}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\mu\nu}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}.

(A.18)

With (
2.5
), (
A.2
) and (
A.4
) this implies

\displaystyle\left(P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda},\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}

(A.19)

\displaystyle=P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{0})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{0},\partial\mathbf{g}_{0})-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\beta}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|^{2}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(-2\Gamma(\mathbf{g}_{0})^{\mu}_{(\alpha\rho}\partial^{\rho}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)\mu}-(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu}\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\beta)\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\beta)}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\mu\nu}\right)\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\beta}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|^{2}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(\pm\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{B}}\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\beta)}^{\sigma}\pm\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{B}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)}^{\sigma}

\displaystyle\pm\frac{1}{2}\partial_{(\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\beta)}u_{\mathbf{B}}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\pm(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{(\alpha L_{\mathbf{B}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\beta)L_{\mathbf{A}}}

\displaystyle\mp\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{A}},\mathrm{d}u_{\mathbf{B}})(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{(\alpha}^{\nu}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\nu\beta)}\bigg),

where we also used (
2.6
). Moreover we also have schematically

\displaystyle\left(P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda},\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}}_{\vcentcolon=A}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(1)}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{-1}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}\left(\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}}_{\vcentcolon=B}.

We start with
A_{\alpha\beta}
. Thanks to (
2.1
) we have

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{\alpha}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)

(A.20)

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{\alpha}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\mu\nu}.

Now, (
2.5
), (
A.18
) and (
A.20
) imply

\displaystyle A_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(\Gamma(\mathbf{g}_{0})^{\mu}_{(\alpha\rho}\partial^{\rho}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left((\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)\mu}+(F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)\mu}\right)+\left\{\partial F^{(1)}+F^{(1)}F^{(2,\pm)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}\Big)

(A.21)

\displaystyle-4\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\Gamma(\mathbf{g}_{0})^{\mu}_{(\alpha\rho}\partial^{\rho}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\beta)\mu}+\left\{F^{(1)}\partial F^{(1)}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u\left\{\left(1+F^{(1)}\right)\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}\right)\right\}_{\beta)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(2,\pm)}+F^{(1)}\left(\partial F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\}_{\alpha\beta}.

It remains to compute
B_{\alpha\beta}
:

\displaystyle B_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(1)}+(F^{(1)})^{3}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(F^{(1)})^{2}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}

(A.22)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{(F^{(1)})^{2}+(F^{(1)})^{3}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u\left\{(F^{(1)})^{3}\right\}_{\beta)}.

Collecting (
A.19
), (
A.21
) and (
A.22
) concludes the proof of Lemma
2.2
.

A.3
Proof of Lemma
2.3

We recall that

\displaystyle H^{\rho}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}\right).

We will first expand the derivatives and then use the expansion of the inverse (see (
A.6
)-(
A.7
)) to obtain
H^{\rho}
. Thanks to (
2.1
) we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(0)}+\lambda D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(2)}+\lambda^{3}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(3)},

where

\displaystyle D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\mu\nu}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right),

\displaystyle D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\mu\nu}\right),

\displaystyle D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial F^{(2,1)}+\partial F^{(2,2)}\right\}_{\mu\nu\sigma}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial F^{(2,\pm)}\right\}_{\mu\nu\sigma}

\displaystyle\quad+\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}u(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}u(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}v(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}v(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\mu\nu}\right),

and

\displaystyle D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\mu\nu}\right).

We have

\displaystyle H^{\rho}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\left(D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(0)}+\lambda D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(2)}+\lambda^{3}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(3)}\right) .

(A.23)

Using the background wave coordinate condition (
1.13
), (
2.6
), (
A.3
) and (
A.5
) we first obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(0)}

(A.24)

\displaystyle=-\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\mu\nu}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\partial^{\rho}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|^{2}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg((F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{\nu}_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nu}^{\rho}\pm(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\nu}_{L_{\mathbf{B}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\nu}^{\rho}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\rho}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\bigg)

\displaystyle-\lambda^{2}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\Bigg(\partial_{\mu}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\mu\nu}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}u_{\mathbf{A}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{1,\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}\right)\left(1+F^{(1)}\right)\right\}^{\rho}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{3}\left\{\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})^{(\geq 3)}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})^{(\geq 1)}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})^{(\geq 2)}\right)\left(1+F^{(1)}\right)\right\}^{\rho,\mathrm{osc}}.

For the remaining terms we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(1)}

(A.25)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\bigg(\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}+\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\mu\nu}\left(\partial_{\mu}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)\bigg)

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}\right]_{\sigma}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{1,\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(1)}\left(\partial F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\}^{\rho}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\Big\{\left((\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 2)}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 1)}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1})^{(\geq 1)}\right)\left(\partial F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}\right)\Big\}^{\rho,\mathrm{osc}}

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(2)}

(A.26)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\mu\nu}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\mu\nu}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T}\middle|u\right]_{\sigma}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\mu}v(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\sigma\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\sigma}v(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\mu\nu}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial F^{(2,1)}+\partial F^{(2,2)}\right\}^{\rho}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial F^{(2,\pm)}\right\}^{\rho}

\displaystyle+\lambda\left\{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})^{(\geq 1)}\left(\partial F^{(2,1)}+\partial F^{(2,2)}+\partial F^{(2,\pm)}+\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}\right)\right\}^{\rho,\mathrm{osc}}.

The term
\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\rho\sigma}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{\mu\nu}D_{\mu\nu\sigma}^{(3)}
is left as it is. Collecting (
A.24
)-(
A.26
) and plugging them into (
A.23
) concludes the proof of Lemma
2.3
.

A.4
Proof of Proposition
2.1

We start with
R^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}
. From (
2.2
), Lemmas
2.1
and
2.2
we have

\displaystyle 2R^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(W^{(0,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(W^{(0,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+P_{\alpha\beta}(\mathbf{g}_{0})(\partial\mathbf{g}_{0},\partial\mathbf{g}_{0})-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\alpha}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\beta}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|^{2}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(P^{(0,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(P^{(0,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(P^{(0,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}+\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(0)}.

We use Lemma
2.3
to compute the gauge terms:

\displaystyle\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(-\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}u_{\mathbf{A}}(H^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}+2\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}u_{\mathbf{A}}(H^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(H^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})^{\rho}.

Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle 2R^{(0)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(2F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\left|F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right|^{2}_{\mathbf{g}_{0}}\right)\partial_{\mu}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\nu}u_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-(W^{(0,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(P^{(0,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}-(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}u_{\mathbf{A}}(H^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((P^{(0,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+2(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}u_{\mathbf{A}}(H^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(-(W^{(0,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}+(P^{(0,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(H^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})^{\rho}\Big),

where we also used (
1.14
). Using now (
2.8
), (
2.15
), (
2.24
), (
2.16
), (
2.25
), (
2.9
) and (
2.26
) we obtain (
2.29
)-(
2.33
).

We now compute
R^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}
. From (
2.2
), Lemmas
2.1
and
2.2
we have

\displaystyle 2R^{(1)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-(W^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(P^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

(A.27)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-(W^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+(P^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(-(W^{(1)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta} +(P^{(1)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u(\hat{P}^{(1)}_{u,T})_{\beta)}+\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(1)}.

We use Lemma
2.3
(and in particular (
2.24
)-(
B.22
)) to compute the gauge terms. We have

\displaystyle\left((\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\left(\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}\right)+\left\{\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(1)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}\right\}^{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}\left(\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}\right)+\left\{F^{(1)}\partial F^{(1)}\right\}^{\rho}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{(\alpha}u

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\bigg(\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T}\middle|u\right]_{\beta)}

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt+\Big\{\partial F^{(2,1)}+\partial F^{(2,2)}

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt+\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}+\partial F^{(1)}\right)\left(1+F^{(1)}\right)\Big\}_{\beta)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\Big\{\partial F^{(2,\pm)}+\left(\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+F^{(2,\pm)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}+\partial F^{(1)}\right)\left(1+F^{(1)}\right)\Big\}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\rho(\alpha}\left(\partial_{\beta)}\Upsilon^{\rho}\right)^{(-1)}

and

\displaystyle\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}+F^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}+\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}F^{(1)}+(F^{(1)})^{3}\right\}_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{0})_{\alpha\beta}\mathbf{g}_{0}^{\rho\sigma}\mathscr{P}\left[F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{\sigma}+\left\{F^{(1)}\left(F^{(2,1)}+\partial^{\leq 1}F^{(1)}\right)\right\}_{\alpha\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\mathscr{P}\left[\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}}\middle|u_{\mathbf{A}}\right]_{L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(F^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{L_{\mathbf{A}}L_{\mathbf{A}}}(F^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{F^{(1)}\left(F^{(1)}+\mathfrak{F}+F^{(2,1)}+F^{(2,2)}+\partial^{\leq 1}F^{(1)}+(F^{(1)})^{2}\right)\right\}_{\alpha\beta}.

We plug these two computations into (
A.27
), use (
2.10
), (
2.18
), (
2.11
), (
2.19
), (
2.12
), (
2.21
) and (
2.20
) and obtain (
2.34
)-(
2.40
).

We now compute
R^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}
. From Lemmas
2.1
and
2.2
we have

\displaystyle 2R^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=-W^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}+P^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}+\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(\geq 2)}.

We use (
2.13
) and (
2.22
) to obtain

\displaystyle 2R^{(\geq 2)}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{h}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{F}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad-\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{N}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{u}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}_{u,T})_{\alpha\beta}-\lambda\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\tilde{\Box}_{\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}}(\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}_{v,T})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big\{\left(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\partial\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\mathbf{g}_{\lambda}^{-1}\big(\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,h)}+\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{g}^{(3,e)}+\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(1)}+\partial^{\leq 1}\mathfrak{F}

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(2,1)}+\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(2,2)}+\partial^{\leq 2}F^{(2,\pm)}\big)\Big\}_{\alpha\beta}^{\mathrm{osc}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(H^{\rho}\partial_{\rho}(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\alpha\beta}+(\mathbf{g}_{\lambda})_{\rho(\alpha}\partial_{\beta)}H^{\rho}\right)^{(\geq 2)},

which gives (
2.41
). This concludes the proof of Proposition
2.1
.

Appendix B
Proof of Proposition
3.1

B.1
Conformal formulation of the constraint equations

We introduce the notations

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}(g,k)

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=R(g)-|k|^{2}_{g}+(\mathrm{tr}_{g}k)^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}(g,k)

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\mathrm{div}_{g}k-\mathrm{d}\mathrm{tr}_{g}k,

In order to solve the constraint equations, we take inspiration from
[
CS06
]
and look for
(g_{\lambda},k_{\lambda})
under the form

\displaystyle g_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\varphi^{4}_{\lambda}\gamma_{\lambda},

(B.1)

\displaystyle k_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\varphi^{2}_{\lambda}\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right),

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{g}X\vcentcolon=\mathcal{L}_{X}g-\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathrm{div}_{g}X\right)g,

(B.2)

with
\mathcal{L}_{X}g
is the Lie derivative. A straightforward computation implies that the constraint equations for
(g_{\lambda},k_{\lambda})
rewrite

\displaystyle 8\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right),

(B.3)

\displaystyle(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda})_{i}

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})_{i}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)_{i},

(B.4)

where

\displaystyle\dot{\Delta}_{g}X_{i}\vcentcolon=\Delta_{g}X_{i}+R_{ij}(g)X^{j}

with
\Delta_{g}
now acting on vector fields and
R_{ij}(g)
the Ricci tensor of
g
, and where the remainders are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=(\varphi_{\lambda}-1)\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\varphi_{\lambda}\bigg(-\left|\mathcal{L}_{X_{\lambda}}\gamma_{\lambda}\right|^{2}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}-2\left|\kappa_{\lambda}\cdot\mathcal{L}_{X_{\lambda}}\gamma_{\lambda}\right|_{\gamma_{\lambda}}

(B.5)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{3}{2}\left(\mathrm{div}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)^{2}+2\left(\mathrm{tr}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)\left(\mathrm{div}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)\bigg),

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)_{i}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=-4\varphi_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{ab}\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathcal{L}_{X_{\lambda}}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)_{ai}\partial_{b}\varphi_{\lambda}+2\varphi_{\lambda}^{-1}\left(\mathrm{div}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)\partial_{i}\varphi_{\lambda}.

(B.6)

The parameters
\gamma_{\lambda}
and
\kappa_{\lambda}
of the conformal formulation are defined by

\displaystyle\gamma_{\lambda}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=g_{0}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}},

(B.7)

\displaystyle\kappa_{\lambda}
