\displaystyle\vcentcolon=k_{0}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\frac{1}{2}|\nabla u_{\mathbf{A}}|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

(B.8)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}.

The unknowns
\varphi_{\lambda}
and
X_{\lambda}
of the conformal formulation are of the form

\displaystyle\varphi_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=1+\lambda^{2}\varphi^{(2)}+\lambda^{2}\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}+\lambda^{3}\varphi^{(3)},

(B.9)

\displaystyle X_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\lambda^{2}X^{(2)}+\lambda^{2}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}+\lambda^{3}X^{(3)}.

(B.10)

where

\displaystyle\varphi^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\varphi^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\varphi^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}},

(B.11)

\displaystyle X^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\left(\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)X^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}.

(B.12)

In (
B.9
)-(
B.10
),
\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}
and
\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}
are non-oscillating remainders, while
\varphi^{(3)}
and
X^{(3)}
are oscillating but we don’t need to be precise on their oscillating behaviour.

B.2
The constraint hierarchy

We first compute useful expansions related to
\gamma_{\lambda}
. The inverse of
\gamma_{\lambda}
satisfies

\displaystyle\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij}=g_{0}^{ij}+\lambda(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij})^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij})^{(2)}+O\left(\lambda^{3}\right)

(B.13)

with

\displaystyle(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij})^{(1)}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{ij},

(B.14)

\displaystyle(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij})^{(2)}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})^{ij}-\sum_{\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})^{ik}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{k}^{j}.

(B.15)

The Christofel symbols of
\gamma_{\lambda}
satisfy

\displaystyle\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{k}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{ij}+(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}+\lambda(\Gamma^{(1)})^{k}_{ij}+O\left(\lambda^{2}\right)

(B.16)

with

\displaystyle(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial_{(i}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{j)}^{k}-\partial^{k}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\right),

(B.17)

\displaystyle(\Gamma^{(1)})^{k}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{Q}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k}_{ij}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)g_{0}^{k\ell}\left(\partial_{(i}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\gamma^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{j)\ell}-\partial_{\ell}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\gamma^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)g_{0}^{k\ell}\Big(\partial_{(i}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{j)\ell}-\partial_{\ell}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{ij}\Big)

(B.18)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k\ell}\left(\partial_{(i}u_{\mathbf{B}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{j)\ell}-\partial_{\ell}u_{\mathbf{B}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{ij}\right),

and

\displaystyle(\bar{Q}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(g_{0}^{k\ell}\left(\partial_{(i}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{j)\ell}-\partial_{\ell}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\right)-(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k\ell}\left(\partial_{(i}(g_{0})_{j)\ell}-\partial_{\ell}(g_{0})_{ij}\right)\right).

Note that (
3.3
) implies

\displaystyle(\bar{Q}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k}_{kj}

\displaystyle=0,

(B.19)

\displaystyle g_{0}^{ij}\partial_{k}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\bar{Q}_{\mathbf{A}})^{k}_{ij}

\displaystyle=(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\left(-\partial^{i}\partial^{j}u_{\mathbf{A}}+\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\ell}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\ell}g_{0}^{ij}\right).

We are now ready to compute the main terms in the equations (
B.3
)-(
B.4
).

Lemma B.1
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}+\lambda\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)},

where

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}

(B.20)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\left(\partial^{i}\partial^{j}u_{\mathbf{A}}-\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\ell}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\ell}g_{0}^{ij}-|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}k_{0}^{ij}\right)

\displaystyle-6\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}-(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(\pm 2(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}}^{i}

\displaystyle+\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}\Big(-2|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}-2|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle\mp 3|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}

\displaystyle\pm|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\Big)\bigg).

Moreover, the higher order terms satisfy

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\left((\partial\gamma_{\lambda})^{2}+(\kappa_{\lambda})^{2}\right)\right)^{(1)}\right\},

(B.21)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\left((\partial\gamma_{\lambda})^{2}+(\kappa_{\lambda})^{2}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

(B.22)

Proof.

We start with the scalar curvature, using its expression in coordinates:

\displaystyle R(\gamma)

\displaystyle=\gamma^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}\Gamma(\gamma)^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}\Gamma(\gamma)^{k}_{jk}+\Gamma(\gamma)^{k}_{k\ell}\Gamma(\gamma)^{\ell}_{ij}-\Gamma(\gamma)^{k}_{i\ell}\Gamma(\gamma)^{\ell}_{jk}\right).

Using (
3.3
) we first obtain

\displaystyle\left(R(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{jk}\right)^{(-1)}.

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}}-|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle=0.

We have
\left(R(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}=I+II+III
with

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{k}_{jk}\right)^{(0)},

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij}\right)^{(1)}\left(\partial_{k}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{jk}\right)^{(-1)},

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{k}_{k\ell}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{\ell}_{ij}-\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{k}_{i\ell}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{\ell}_{jk}\right)^{(0)}.

We start with
I
. Using
(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{jk}=0
and
g_{0}^{ij}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}=0
(which follow from (
B.17
) and (
3.3
)) we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{jk}\right)-(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}\partial_{k}g_{0}^{ij}+g_{0}^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}(\Gamma^{(1)})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}(\Gamma^{(1)})^{k}_{jk}\right)^{(-1)}

(B.23)

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\partial_{k}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{ij}-\partial_{i}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{jk}\right)-(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{ij}\partial_{k}g_{0}^{ij}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\left(\partial^{i}\partial^{j}u_{\mathbf{A}}-\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\ell}u_{\mathbf{A}}\partial_{\ell}g_{0}^{ij}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(|\nabla u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\gamma^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-(\gamma^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\Big(|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}-(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})}\Big)

\displaystyle-4\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}},

where we have also used (
B.18
) and (
B.19
). We now compute
II
, using (
B.14
) and (
B.17
):

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}-2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

(B.24)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}+|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}\right)\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}.

We now compute
III
using (
B.17
):

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{k\ell}\Gamma(g_{0})^{\ell}_{ij}-\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{i\ell}\Gamma(g_{0})^{\ell}_{jk}\right)-2g_{0}^{ij}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{\ell}_{ik}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{j\ell}-g_{0}^{ij}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{k}_{i\ell}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{\ell}_{jk}

(B.25)

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ij}\left(\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{k\ell}\Gamma(g_{0})^{\ell}_{ij}-\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{i\ell}\Gamma(g_{0})^{\ell}_{jk}\right)-2g_{0}^{ij}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{\ell}_{ik}\Gamma(g_{0})^{k}_{j\ell}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(\pm 2(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}}^{i}

\displaystyle\mp|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\left| \bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}\bigg).

We now compute the
\lambda^{0}
contribution of the quadratic terms in
\kappa_{\lambda}
. From (
3.3
) we have
\mathrm{tr}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\kappa_{\lambda}=\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}k_{0}+O\left(\lambda\right)
so that

\displaystyle\left(\left(\mathrm{tr}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)^{2}\right)^{(0)}=(\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}k_{0})^{2}

(B.26)

and

\displaystyle\left(|\kappa_{\lambda}|^{2}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\right)^{(0)}

\displaystyle=|k_{0}|^{2}_{g_{0}}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot k_{0}\right|_{g_{0}}

(B.27)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}^{2}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\mp\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}.

Since the quadratic terms in
\kappa_{\lambda}
don’t contribute to
\lambda^{-1}
order we have proved that

\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(-1)}=0.

Moreover we have also proved that
\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}
is indeed given by (
B.20
), putting together (
B.23
)-(
B.27
) and using in particular (
1.20
) and (
1.24
) to cancel the resonant term. It only remains to prove (
B.21
), since (
B.22
) follows from the definition of the Hamiltonian constraint operator
\mathcal{H}
. In order to prove (
B.21
), we only need to check that
\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
is purely oscillating, i.e that is does not contain any resonant term. We use a schematic expression of
\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
which follows from the definition of
\mathcal{H}
:

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(0)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}+\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(1)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}+\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(2)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(-1)}

(B.28)

\displaystyle+\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}+\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}.

We list every oscillating functions appearing in the five terms of the schematic expression (
B.28
):

•

Thanks to (
B.13
),
\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(0)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}
contains
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)
and
\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
.

•

Thanks to (
B.14
)-(
B.15
),
\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(1)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}
and
\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(2)}\left(\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(-1)}
contain
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm 2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
and
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm_{1}u_{\mathbf{B}}\pm_{2}u_{\mathbf{C}}}{\lambda}\right)
.

•

Thanks to (
B.17
)-(
B.18
),
\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
contains
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
,
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm 2u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)
and
\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm_{1}u_{\mathbf{B}}\pm_{2}u_{\mathbf{C}}}{\lambda}\right)
.

•

Thanks to (
B.7
)-(
B.8
) we see that the oscillating behaviour of
\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}
is the same as the oscillating behaviour of
\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})
so that
\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)^{(0)}\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}
behaves as
\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}\left(\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
.

This concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma B.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)}+\lambda\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)},

where

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})_{i}\right)^{(0)}

(B.29)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-(\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\partial^{b}\left(|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)_{bi}+\frac{1}{4}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{bc}\partial_{i}g_{0}^{bc}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\left(\partial_{a}g_{0}^{ca}+\frac{1}{2}g_{0}^{ab}g_{0}^{cd}\partial_{d}(g_{0})_{ab}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ic}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{bc}(k_{0})^{bc}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(2(\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}-2\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}+3|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{(\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}})i}-\partial_{i}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\bigg((\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{b}_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{bi}-\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{B}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}\bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}.

Moreover, the higher order terms satisfy

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\kappa_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}\right\}

(B.30)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\kappa_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

(B.31)

Proof.

In coordinates we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}(g,k)_{i}

\displaystyle=g^{ab}\left(\partial_{a}k_{bi}-\partial_{i}k_{ab}\right)+\left(\partial_{a}g^{ca}+\frac{1}{2}g^{ab}g^{cd}\partial_{d}g_{ab}\right)k_{ic}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}g^{bc}k_{bc}.

Using again (
3.3
) we easily obtain
\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})_{i}\right)^{(-1)}=0
. We now have
\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})_{i}\right)^{(0)}=I+II+III
where

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=g_{0}^{ab}\left(\partial_{a}(\kappa_{\lambda})_{bi}-\partial_{i}(\kappa_{\lambda})_{ab}\right)^{(0)},

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=(\gamma_{\lambda}^{ab})^{(1)}\left(\partial_{a}(\kappa_{\lambda})_{bi}-\partial_{i}(\kappa_{\lambda})_{ab}\right)^{(-1)},

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left(\partial_{a}\gamma_{\lambda}^{ca}+\frac{1}{2}g_{0}^{ab}g_{0}^{cd}\partial_{d}(\gamma_{\lambda})_{ab}\right)^{(0)}\left((\kappa_{\lambda})_{ic}\right)^{(0)}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial_{i}\gamma_{\lambda}^{bc}\right)^{(0)}\left((\kappa_{\lambda})_{bc}\right)^{(0)}.

We start with
I
:

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{ab}\left(\partial_{a}(k_{0})_{bi}-\partial_{i}(k_{0})_{ab}\right)

(B.32)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-(\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial^{b}\left(|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)_{bi}+|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ab}\partial_{i}g_{0}^{ab}\right)\bigg)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}-\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left((\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{(\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}})i}-\partial_{i}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\right).

We now compute
II
and
III
using (
B.14
):

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}+2\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

(B.33)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\left((\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})^{b}_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}})_{bi}-\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{B}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}\right).

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{a}g_{0}^{ca}+\frac{1}{2}g_{0}^{ab}g_{0}^{cd}\partial_{d}(g_{0})_{ab}\right)(k_{0})_{ic}-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}g_{0}^{bc}(k_{0})_{bc}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bigg(|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\left(\partial_{a}g_{0}^{ca}+\frac{1}{2}g_{0}^{ab}g_{0}^{cd}\partial_{d}(g_{0})_{ab}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ic}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt-\frac{1}{2}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{bc}\partial_{i}g_{0}^{bc}-\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{bc}(k_{0})^{bc}\bigg)

(B.34)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\left|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\cdot\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right|_{g_{0}}.

Collecting (
B.32
)-(
B.34
) and using (
1.21
) and (
1.24
) to cancel the non-oscillating term we have proved that
\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})_{i}\right)^{(0)}
is indeed given by (
B.29
). It only remains to prove (
B.30
), since (
B.31
) follows from the definition of the momentum constraint operator
\mathcal{M}
. This actually follows from (
B.21
) since
\kappa_{\lambda}
has the same oscillating behaviour than a derivative of
\gamma_{\lambda}
. In particular
\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
and
\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}
share the same oscillating behaviour. This concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We now estimate the remainders in (
B.3
)-(
B.4
).

Lemma B.3
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=\lambda\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)},

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=\lambda\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}+\lambda^{2}\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)},

where

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(1+\bar{F}^{(1)}\right)\left(X^{(2,1)}+X^{(2,2)}+X^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\},

(B.35)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}

\displaystyle=\left(\varphi^{(2)}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}+\lambda\varphi^{(3)}\right)\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)

(B.36)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\Big\{\left(\varphi_{\lambda}(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1})^{2}\left(\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda}\right)(\kappa_{\lambda}+\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)}\Big\}^{\mathrm{osc}},

and

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\left(1+\bar{F}^{(1)}\right)\left(\varphi^{(2,1)}+\varphi^{(2,2)}+\varphi^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\},

(B.37)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\varphi_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\varphi_{\lambda}(\kappa_{\lambda}+\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

(B.38)

Proof.

We start with
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)
defined in (
B.5
). Since
\varphi_{\lambda}-1=O\left(\lambda^{2}\right)
(see (
B.9
)) and
\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)=O\left(1\right)
(see Lemma
B.1
),
(\varphi_{\lambda}-1)\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)
does not contribute to
\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}
. We find that
\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}
is of the schematic form

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\varphi_{\lambda}(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1})^{2}\left(\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda}\right)(\kappa_{\lambda}+\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=(g_{0}^{-1})^{2}\left(\partial X_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}(\kappa_{\lambda})^{(0)}

where we also used
\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda}=O\left(\lambda^{2}\right)
and
\partial X_{\lambda}=O\left(\lambda\right)
. Using now
\left(\partial X_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}=\left(\partial X^{(2)}\right)^{(-1)}
, (
B.12
) and (
B.8
) we finally obtain (
B.35
). Moreover, (
B.36
) can be deduced from the schematic expression of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)
. We now turn to
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)
defined in (
B.6
).

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\varphi_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\varphi_{\lambda}(\kappa_{\lambda}+\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}X_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}

\displaystyle=g_{0}^{-1}\left(\partial\varphi_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}(\kappa_{\lambda})^{(0)}

where we used
\partial\varphi_{\lambda}=O\left(\lambda\right)
. Using now
\left(\partial\varphi_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}=\left(\partial\varphi^{(2)}\right)^{(-1)}
, (
B.11
) and (
B.8
) we finally obtain (
B.37
). Moreover, (
B.36
) can be deduced from the schematic expression of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)
. This concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Finally we expand the elliptic operators appearing on the RHS of (
B.3
)-(
B.4
).

Lemma B.4
.

Let
f
and
v
be scalar functions and
Z
a vector field on
\Sigma_{0}
and let
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}
.

(i)

We have

\displaystyle\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}f

\displaystyle=\Delta_{e}f+\left\{(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}-e^{-1})\partial^{2}f+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\partial f\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

(B.39)

and

\displaystyle\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)f\right)\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=T^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}v|^{2}_{g_{0}}f,

(B.40)

\displaystyle\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)f\right)\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}f\right\}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\bar{F}^{(1)}f\right\},

(B.41)

\displaystyle\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)f\right)\right)^{(\geq 0)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}f+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}\partial f\right)^{(\geq 0)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

(B.42)

(ii)

We have

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}Z_{i}\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}Z^{j},

(B.43)

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}Z_{i}\right)^{(\geq 0)}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{e}Z_{i}+\Big\{(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}-e^{-1})\partial^{2}Z+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\partial Z

(B.44)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\left(\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 0)}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)Z\Big\}^{\mathrm{osc}}_{i}.

and

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)Z\right)_{i}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=T^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}v|^{2}_{g_{0}}Z_{i}

(B.45)

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)Z\right)_{i}\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{\partial^{\leq 1}Z\right\}_{i}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left\{Z\bar{F}^{(1)}\right\}_{i}

(B.46)

and

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)Z\right)_{i}\right)^{(\geq 0)}

(B.47)

\displaystyle=\Big\{\left(\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 1)}+\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 0)}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)Z

\displaystyle+\left(\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\right)^{(\geq 1)}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})\right)\partial^{\leq 1}Z+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}Z\Big\}_{i}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

Proof.

The identity (
B.39
) follows directly from the expression of the Laplace-Beltrami operator in coordinates. The following computation is the elliptic equivalent of (
A.8
):

\displaystyle\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)f\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}T^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}(\mathrm{d}v,\mathrm{d}v)f+\frac{1}{\lambda}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(2\gamma_{\lambda}^{ij}\partial_{j}v\partial_{i}f+(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}v)f\right)+T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}f.

We use the expansion of the inverse of
\gamma_{\lambda}
given by (
B.13
) to obtain (
B.40
)-(
B.42
). We now turn to the elliptic operator
\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}
acting on vector fields. Thanks to the expression of the Ricci tensor in coordinates we find

\displaystyle\dot{\Delta}_{g}Z_{i}

\displaystyle=g^{k\ell}\partial_{k}\partial_{\ell}Z_{i}-2g^{k\ell}\Gamma(g)_{\ell i}^{a}\partial_{k}Z_{a}-g^{k\ell}Z_{a}\left(\partial_{k}\Gamma(g)_{\ell i}^{a}-\Gamma(g)_{ki}^{b}\Gamma(g)_{\ell b}^{a}\right)

(B.48)

\displaystyle+Z^{j}\partial_{a}\Gamma(g)^{a}_{ij}-Z^{j}\partial_{j}\Gamma(g)^{a}_{ai}+Z^{j}\Gamma(g)^{a}_{ab}\Gamma(g)^{b}_{ij}-Z^{j}\Gamma(g)^{a}_{ib}\Gamma(g)^{b}_{aj}.

This already gives

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}Z_{i}\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=Z_{b}\left(-g_{0}^{k\ell}\partial_{k}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})_{\ell i}^{b}+g_{0}^{jb}\left(\partial_{a}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{a}_{ij}-\partial_{j}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(0)})^{a}_{ai}\right)\right)^{(-1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}Z^{j}.

Moreover this also gives (
B.44
). Thanks to (
B.48
) we finally obtain

\displaystyle\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\left(T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)Z\right)_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}T^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{k}v\partial_{\ell}vZ_{i}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\lambda}T^{\prime}\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(2\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}\partial_{\ell}v\partial_{k}Z_{i}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}\partial_{k}\partial_{\ell}vZ_{i}-2\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})_{\ell i}^{a}\partial_{k}vZ_{a}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(Z^{j}\partial_{a}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{a}_{ij}-Z^{j}\partial_{j}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{a}_{ai}-\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}Z_{a}\partial_{k}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})_{\ell i}^{a}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}Z_{a}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})_{ki}^{b}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})_{\ell b}^{a}+Z^{j}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{a}_{ab}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{b}_{ij}-Z^{j}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{a}_{ib}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})^{b}_{aj}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+T\left(\frac{v}{\lambda}\right)\left(-2\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}\Gamma(\gamma_{\lambda})_{\ell i}^{a}\partial_{k}Z_{a} +\gamma_{\lambda}^{k\ell}\partial_{k}\partial_{\ell}Z_{i}\right).

We use the expansion of the inverse of
\gamma_{\lambda}
given by (
B.13
) and of the Christofel symbols given by (
B.16
) to deduce (
B.45
)-(
B.47
). This concludes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We are now ready to define the constraint hierarchy, i.e to deduce from Lemmas
B.1
-
B.4
a hierarchy of equations for the various terms in
\varphi_{\lambda}
and
X_{\lambda}
such that they solve (
B.3
)-(
B.4
). The hierarchy associated to the Hamiltonian constraint is

\displaystyle 8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(2)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)},

(B.49)

\displaystyle 8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(3)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{H}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}+\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}-8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(2)}\right)^{(-1)},

(B.50)

\displaystyle 8\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{H}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}-8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(2)}\right)^{(\geq 0)}

(B.51)

\displaystyle\quad-8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(3)}\right)^{(\geq-1)}.

The hierarchy associated to the momentum constraint is

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(2)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=-\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(0)},

(B.52)

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(3)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=-\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(1)}+\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(1)}-\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(2)}\right)^{(-1)}

(B.53)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}\right)^{(-1)},

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 0)}

\displaystyle=-\left(\mathcal{M}(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda})\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\left(\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{M}}\left(\gamma_{\lambda},\kappa_{\lambda},\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}-\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(2)}\right)^{(\geq 0)}

(B.54)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(3)}\right)^{(\geq-1)}.

Remark B.1
.

The momentum hierarchy (
B.52
)-(
B.54
) differs from the Hamiltonian hierarchy (
B.49
)-(
B.51
) because of the term
\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}\right)^{(-1)}
in (
B.53
). The presence of this term is due to the fact that the operator
\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}
acting on vector fields contains second derivatives of
\gamma_{\lambda}
and thus looses one power of
\lambda
even when applied to a non-oscillating vector field such as
\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}
. This is not the case for the operator
\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}
acting on functions since it only contains up to first derivatives of
\gamma_{\lambda}
.

B.3
Solving the hierarchy

In this section we construct a solution
\left(\varphi^{(2)},\varphi^{(3)},\widetilde{\varphi},X^{(2)},X^{(3)},\widetilde{X}\right)
of the equations (
B.49
)-(
B.54
).

The
\lambda^{0}
equations.

We start by defining
\varphi^{(2)}
and
X^{(2)}
such that they solve (
B.49
) and (
B.52
). On the one hand, (
B.11
)-(
B.12
) together with (
B.40
) and (
B.45
) imply

\displaystyle 8\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(2)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=-8\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}\varphi^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-32\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}\varphi^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\quad-8\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}},

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(2)}\right)^{(-2)}_{i}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}(X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}-4\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}(X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}(X^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{i}.

On the other hand, (
B.20
) and (
B.29
) give the expression of the RHS of (
B.49
) and (
B.52
). Therefore, solving (
B.49
) and (
B.52
) is equivalent to defining
\varphi^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
\varphi^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
by

\displaystyle\varphi^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\widecheck{\varphi}^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}},

(B.55)

\displaystyle\varphi^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\frac{3}{16}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2},

(B.56)

\displaystyle\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=-\frac{1}{8}\left(\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}-(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{N^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}N^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}}\right)+\widecheck{\varphi}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}},

(B.57)

and
(X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}
,
(X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}
,
(X^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{i}
by

\displaystyle(X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\frac{1}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}}\left(\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-(\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}+(\widecheck{X}^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}\right),

(B.58)

\displaystyle(X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{i}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\frac{1}{4|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}}\left(2(\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}i}-2\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}+3|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}\partial_{i}u_{\mathbf{A}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\right),

(B.59)

\displaystyle(X^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{i}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\frac{1}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})|^{2}_{g_{0}}}\left((\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{(\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{B}})i}-\partial_{i}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\mathrm{tr}_{g_{0}}\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}+(\widecheck{X}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}})_{i}\right).

(B.60)

where we recognized the expression of
\widecheck{\varphi}^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
\widecheck{\varphi}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
,
\widecheck{X}^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\widecheck{X}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
given by (
3.13
)-(
3.16
).

The
\lambda^{1}
equations.

We now define
\varphi^{(3)}
and
X^{(3)}
such that they solve (
B.50
) and (
B.53
). Thanks to (
B.21
), (
B.35
), (
B.11
) and (
B.41
) the equation (
B.50
) rewrites

\displaystyle\left(\Delta_{\gamma_{\lambda}}\varphi^{(3)}\right)^{(-2)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)S^{\mathcal{H}}_{w,T},

(B.61)

where each
S^{\mathcal{H}}_{w,T}
is of the form

\displaystyle S^{\mathcal{H}}_{w,T}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial^{2}\gamma_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\left((\partial\gamma_{\lambda})^{2}+(\kappa_{\lambda})^{2}\right)\right)^{(1)}+\partial\left(\varphi^{(2,1)}+\varphi^{(2,2)}+\varphi^{(2,\pm)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\left(1+\bar{F}^{(1)}\right)\left(X^{(2,1)}+X^{(2,2)}+X^{(2,\pm)}+\varphi^{(2,1)}+\varphi^{(2,2)}+\varphi^{(2,\pm)}\right)\bigg\}.

We now define
\varphi^{(3)}
by

\displaystyle\varphi^{(3)}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\frac{S^{\mathcal{H}}_{w,T}}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}w|^{2}_{g_{0}}}.

(B.62)

Using (
B.40
) and
T^{\prime\prime}=-T
for
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}
one can check that
\varphi^{(3)}
solves (
B.61
) and thus (
B.50
). We now turn to (
B.53
), which thanks to (
B.30
), (
B.37
), (
B.12
), (
B.43
) and (
B.46
) rewrites

\displaystyle\left(\dot{\Delta}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X^{(3)}\right)^{(-2)}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)(S^{\mathcal{M}}_{w,T})_{i}-\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|^{2}_{g_{0}}(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}^{j},

(B.63)

where each
S^{\mathcal{M}}_{w,T}
is of the form

\displaystyle S^{\mathcal{M}}_{w,T}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\left(\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\partial\kappa_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\kappa_{\lambda}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}\right)^{(1)}+\partial\left(X^{(2,1)}+X^{(2,2)}+X^{(2,\pm)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left(1+\bar{F}^{(1)}\right)\left(X^{(2,1)}+X^{(2,2)}+X^{(2,\pm)}+\varphi^{(2,1)}+\varphi^{(2,2)}+\varphi^{(2,\pm)}\right)\bigg\}.

In order to solve this equation, we further decompose
X^{(3)}
into

\displaystyle X^{(3)}_{i}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{I}\\
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}\end{subarray}}T\left(\frac{w}{\lambda}\right)\frac{(S^{\mathcal{M}}_{w,T})_{i}}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}w|^{2}_{g_{0}}}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)(\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}})_{ij}\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}^{j}.

(B.64)

Using (
B.45
) and
T^{\prime\prime}=-T
for
T\in\{\cos,\sin\}
one can check that
X^{(3)}
solves (
B.63
) and thus (
B.53
). Following Remark
B.1
, note that
X^{(3)}
depends on the remainder
\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}
.

The
\lambda^{2}
equations.

We conclude the resolution of (
B.3
)-(
B.4
) by solving for
\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}
and
\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}
. This step is identical to the singlephase case considered in
[
Tou23b
]
since the multiphase aspect of the current construction does not change the nature of the equations for the remainders. We point out two harmless difference:

•

The conformal formulation here is slightly different from the one used in
[
Tou23b
]
. In particular the elliptic operator for the vector equation is different but as in
[
Tou23b
]
we will benefit from (
B.44
) and invert
\Delta_{e}
.

•

The free tensors
\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
,
\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}
,
\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}
and
\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}
are not strictly speaking present in
[
Tou23b
]
but they can easily be estimated with the assumption (
3.10
).

Therefore, following the same steps as Section 6 of
[
Tou23b
]
we obtain the existence of a unique couple
\left(\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda},\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}\right)\in\left(H^{N-3}_{\delta}\right)^{2}
solving (
B.3
)-(
B.4
) and satisfying moreover

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}\right\|_{H^{k+2}_{\delta}}+\left\|\widetilde{X}_{\lambda}\right\|_{H^{k+2}_{\delta}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{\lambda^{k}}

(B.65)

for
k\in\llbracket 0,N-5\rrbracket
.

B.4
Final form of the solution

In the previous section we have a constructed a solution
(\varphi_{\lambda},X_{\lambda})
to the system (
B.3
)-(
B.4
). Therefore we have constructed a solution of the constraint equations
(g_{\lambda},k_{\lambda})
of the form (
B.1
). To conclude the proof of Proposition
3.1
we need to recover the expressions (
3.11
)-(
3.12
) as well as the estimates (
3.17
)-(
3.18
).

The induced metric.

We have
g_{\lambda}=\varphi_{\lambda}^{4}\gamma_{\lambda}
, so that (
B.7
) and (
B.9
) implies

\displaystyle g_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=g_{0}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+4\lambda^{2}\left(\varphi^{(2)}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}\right)g_{0}+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{3}\left\{\left(\varphi^{(2)}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}+\left(\varphi_{\lambda}^{4}\right)^{(\geq 3)}\right)\left(\bar{F}^{(1)}+\gamma^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

Using now (
B.11
) we obtain

\displaystyle g_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=g_{0}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)4\varphi^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}g_{0}+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)4\varphi^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}g_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}+4\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}g_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{2}\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}g_{0}+\lambda^{3}\left\{\left(\varphi^{(2)}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}+\left(\varphi_{\lambda}^{4}\right)^{(\geq 3)}\right)\left(\bar{F}^{(1)}+\gamma^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

Using now (
B.57
) we obtain

\displaystyle\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}+4\varphi^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}g_{0}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[1]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}\left(\gamma^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\right)+4\widecheck{\varphi}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}g_{0},

where we recognized the operator
\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[1]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}
defined by (
3.5
). Using also (
B.55
) and (
B.56
), and defining

\displaystyle h_{\lambda}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}g_{0}+\lambda\left\{\left(\varphi^{(2)}+\widetilde{\varphi}_{\lambda}+\left(\varphi_{\lambda}^{4}\right)^{(\geq 3)}\right)\left(\bar{F}^{(1)}+\gamma^{(2,\pm)}\right)\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}

allows us to recover the expression (
3.11
) for
g_{\lambda}
. The estimate (
3.17
) follows from (
3.4
), (
3.10
), the definition of
\varphi^{(2)}
and
\varphi^{(3)}
and (
B.65
).

The second fundamental form.

We have

\displaystyle k_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\varphi^{2}_{\lambda}\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right).

We first expand
\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}
. Thanks to (
B.2
), (
B.10
) and (
B.12
) we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}-2\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\quad-\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{2}\left\{\left(\left(1+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}\gamma_{\lambda}\right)\left(\partial X_{\lambda}+\gamma_{\lambda}^{-1}X_{\lambda}\partial\gamma_{\lambda}\right)\right)^{(\geq 2)}\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}},

where we also used the notation introduced in (
3.9
). Using now also (
B.8
) we obtain

\displaystyle k_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=k_{0}+\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\frac{1}{2}|\nabla u_{\mathbf{A}}|\bar{F}^{(1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\cos\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}}\sin\left(\frac{2u_{\mathbf{A}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}-2\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda\sum_{\mathbf{A}\neq\mathbf{B},\pm}\sin\left(\frac{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}-\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{2}\left\{\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\left(\varphi^{2}_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}.

We use now (
B.58
)-(
B.60
) to obtain

\displaystyle\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}+\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[2]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}}\left(\kappa^{(1,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)+\frac{N_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}\widecheck{X}^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}},

\displaystyle\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}-2\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[2]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}}\left(\kappa^{(1,2)}_{\mathbf{A}}\right)-\frac{3}{2}|\nabla_{g_{0}}u_{\mathbf{A}}|_{g_{0}}F_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}N_{\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\otimes}N_{\mathbf{A}},

\displaystyle\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}-\nabla_{g_{0}}(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}})\tilde{\otimes}X^{(2,1)}_{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[2]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}\left(\kappa^{(1,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\right)-\frac{N^{(\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}\tilde{\otimes}\widecheck{X}^{(2,\pm)}_{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}}}{|\nabla_{g_{0}}\left(u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}\right)|_{g_{0}}},

where we recognized the operators
\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[2]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}}
and
\bar{\mathcal{P}}^{[2]}_{u_{\mathbf{A}}\pm u_{\mathbf{B}}}
defined by (
3.6
). Defining

\displaystyle\tilde{k}^{cons}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left\{\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}+\left(\varphi^{2}_{\lambda}\right)^{(\geq 2)}\left(\kappa_{\lambda}+\mathrm{L}_{\gamma_{\lambda}}X_{\lambda}\right)\right\}^{\mathrm{osc}}

allows us to recover the expression (
3.12
) for
k_{\lambda}
. The estimate (
3.18
) follows from (
3.4
), (
3.10
), the definition of
\varphi^{(2)}
,
\varphi^{(3)}
,
X^{(2)}
,
X^{(3)}
and (
B.65
).
